2026-10-31T12:30:40.3797163Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [prefill-pp4] Serve PP4 round 12 with pair-only M192/N96 Prefill FA3 behind its 12-GPU in-job gate

Round 12 changes one thing against round 10 (41150387984, 9896.09): the
PP4 config sets prefill.fa3_pair_gate, and the fresh image carries the
M192/N96 native FA3 row (ported from 821f409/fd8a66c by komoriyuta) next
to the served n192-direct-o-varlen-pipe row.

Dispatch: with SGLANG_PREFILL_FA3_VERIFY set to
n192-direct-o-varlen-pipe+m192n96-pair, a fixed Prefill FA3 call uses
M192/N96 only when the host-side extend lengths hold exactly two requests
of 3900-4096 tokens each, which is the steady-state PP4 microbatch.
Everything else keeps the served row, whose SASS is byte-identical to
before. The value is never pinned by hand: the env starts at the control
and only the gate rewrites it.

Gate, on all twelve Prefill H200s before model load: seven pair shapes
(3994+3994, 3986+4027, 3986+3986, 4027+4027, 4096+4096, 3900+4096,
4096+3901) against the FP32 causal reference with the existing
tolerances and changed-input replays; paired producer+FA3 timing in both
orders with at least 5 us saved per call on every GPU and shape; then
1200 serving-path calls at 3994+3994 with a numeric re-check and byte
equality to its own first output. Any failure or timeout keeps the
control and serving continues.

Why: in 41151283746 the all-shape M192/N96 screen saved 17.9-27.3 us on
every two-request cell on all twelve GPUs, about 0.43-0.65 ms per
24-layer period, but control served because one single-request cell saved
only 4.5 us against the 5 us rule. Single requests now never reach the
new row, so the rule stays unchanged. Expected +0.35-0.5 percent if
adopted. The image pin in the config is left at round 10: the fresh-build
provision rewrites the runtime config with the new image, as round 10
